## Four evidence registers and field shapes

1. Philological descent: linguistic history requires attested forms and responsible reconstruction.
2. Attested semantic field: actual usage in a dated text/language; historical leaves need their own documents.
3. Operational homology / authorial construction: exact operation, transformation, consequence and return; does not claim common ancestry.
4. Poetic / phonic re-entry: consciously held resonance; no philological, mathematical or empirical proof transfers.

N / N′ → N+N′ means N generated relational determinations, not concatenation. N→N×N distributive recursion is different. 3:3 and 4:2 are orienting readings. Root/coordinate→stem→branch→leaf→fruit→seed is generative description, not mandatory morphology. Fullness is maximum earned articulation with recursive return.

| Field | Earned shape |
|---|---|
| E1 | Six names; HISTORY language-unit includes parent / = −/− and 1/0 return; four explicitly named consumed operations. |
| E2 | 6 / 6′ → six generated relations; downstream lexical flowerings are not replacement relation names. Not twelve and not 6×6. |
| E3 | Six offices Fides#0 Topos#1 Logos#2 Nomos#3 Natio#4 Credere#5→Fides; internal fourfold Topos→Logos→Nomos→Natio. |
| E4 | Three terms, four operations, source→Symbol→Account→Trust→encounter/revision. |
| E5 | Dyadic relation; agreement, proportion and research boundary are three operational offices, not a new forced threefold census. |
| E6 | Dyadic field with five named applications: finite power, measure, labour, commons, planetary economy. |
| pending | earth-taste-wisdom/HISTORY.md is not a seventh mature WHOLE-FIELD. |

**Consequence:** Keep belonging's historical production visible when an achieved arrangement presents itself as native origin.

**Consumers:** A35, C59, M43, M48.

**Return:** Return a birth-story to its institutions, languages, exclusions and affected people.

**Next development:** Develop the twelve nation-history branches individually; nation, culture and commons retain different bearers.

### E3-credere — Credere (#5) → entrusting toward unresolved return

**Consequence:** Locate liability and renewed commitment where no account guarantees the future.

**Consumers:** A23, A25, A31, C48, M18, M46.

**Return:** Returned performance qualifies subsequent trust rather than proving the source possessed.

**Next development:** Source creditum/credere and the Odyssey surety episode in its whole narrative; no universal history follows from the image.

**Occurrences:** [1–45](</Users/admin/Central/Work/O-I/Antykathera-Essay-Work/submission-package/essay/symbolon/episteme/etymologies/trust-place-logos-nomos-natio-credere/WHOLE-FIELD.md:1>), [93–112](</Users/admin/Central/Work/O-I/Antykathera-Essay-Work/submission-package/essay/symbolon/episteme/etymologies/trust-place-logos-nomos-natio-credere/HISTORY.md:93>).

**Return:** The fruit becomes seed in a wider relational field; C51 returns through compassion and cultural practice to its own conditions.

**Consumers:** A15, A22, A27, A33, C10, C27, C33, C38, M03, M41, M47.

**Return:** A challenge reaches the reference, criterion and permission underlying the account.

**Consumers:** A23, A25, A31, C48, M04, M18, M46.

**Return:** Return can revise the terms of entrustment, not only judge the final output.

**Next development:** Lexical trust/credit history cannot establish the formal limit; independent source and authorial warrants must be retained.

### A01

[A01-Subject-God-and-Faithful-Definition.md](</Users/admin/Central/Work/O-I/Antykathera-Essay-Work/submission-package/essay/symbolon/episteme/arguments/A01-Subject-God-and-Faithful-Definition.md>) · `E4-symbol` · register3.

**Next:** Preserve the accepted depth packet and authorial F corrections; verify source/return links rather than restage the proof.

**Return:** Return through source relation without relinquishing the sign's exactness.

**Actual body witness [62–62](</Users/admin/Central/Work/O-I/Antykathera-Essay-Work/submission-package/essay/symbolon/episteme/arguments/A01-Subject-God-and-Faithful-Definition.md:62>):**

> The [Symbol / Account / Trust whole field](../etymologies/symbol-account-and-trust/WHOLE-FIELD.md) grounds this relation: a determinate Symbol discloses; an Account makes the disclosure answerable; Trust permits committed use while retaining the possibility of encounter, consequence and revision. A01 consumes this as **evidence register 3, operational homology**. It makes no claim of common philological descent among “God,” “Subject,” “definition,” “symbol,” “account” and “trust.” Here the account answers to source without substituting itself for source.

> The [Encounter / Region / Name / Count / Countenance / Account whole field](../etymologies/encounter-region-name-count/WHOLE-FIELD.md) grounds this passage from situated encounter into accountable identification. Its operation keeps address active within counting. A person can count as one participant without becoming exhausted by the count. [A08, Constitutive Exclusion](A08-Apoha-Constitutive-Exclusion.md), qualifies the naming act: deciding which instance counts also excludes alternatives, and those exclusions remain conditions of the decision.

### T21-M01

> ## Warrant
> [[Antikythera Agentworld Brief]] turns agency into a context-bound problem and intensifies the question: an artificial agent cannot be understood solely as an object executing instructions; it inhabits a constructed context-world in which distinctions matter, actions alter future distinctions, and evaluation is internal to a horizon. [[diaphaneity|Jean Gebser]] situates the crisis as a mutation signal: perspectival optics organised a world through the positioned viewer; Internet and AI make the contextual conditions of intelligence—prompt, memory, retrieval, policy, permissions, provenance, and feedback—operatively constitutive. “My view of the world” must eventually become readable in the reverse direction—as the world's disclosure of a situated self. This is both the historical claim and the methodological demand of diaphaneity; its formal geometry and technical consequences are deliberately withheld.

**Exact alignment scope:** Preserve the withheld mechanism and located horizon; add exact A01/A03/A26 return rather than expand the opening into a technical inventory.

**Return:** Movement → named restored A/C bodies → exact E operation and HISTORY/source → revised Movement at its existing position. No copied full field or new linear sequence.

### T21-M22

[22-s2-p3-ares-aphrodite-harmonia.md](</Users/admin/Central/Work/O-I/Antykathera-Essay-Work/submission-package/essay/section-rooms/03-two-logics/movements/22-s2-p3-ares-aphrodite-harmonia.md>) · `E3-credere` · A: A13, A18, A20 · C: C23, C24, C25, C33, C36, C50, C61.

> The scene's ending carries the trust argument in narrative form, and it must be given whole. The completed apparatus cannot conclude. Detection, capture, proof, judgment — every station succeeds, and the episode stalls: the lovers stay bound, the grievance stays open, and the demand for restitution has no mechanism inside the net for being met; a trap can hold a debtor but cannot make a debt good. Release enters from outside the apparatus, with the one god who does not laugh (8.344). Poseidon offers ἐγγύη — "Loose him; I promise he shall pay all that is right among the immortal gods" (8.346–48) — and, when refused, converts the pledge into personal liability: if Ares flees the debt, "I will myself pay thee" (8.355–56). Hephaestus voices the apparatus's own distrust of exactly this relation — "wretched are the sureties taken for the wretched" (8.351) — and accepts it anyway, because nothing else can open the bonds and close the account. A person standing-for another at real risk of loss, a relation no mechanism generated, is what the mechanism's maker must borrow to finish what his technē began. [[Trust, Faith, and the Formal Limit]] receives this as its mythic proof-case, and [[40-s5-p3-preference-hidden-zero|§5 · #3]] receives its technical form: no alignment system is trustless, and the finished score conceals the pledge that underwrites it.

**Exact alignment scope:** Preserve the entire Homeric apparatus and surety release, with Hesiod's birth separately sourced. Add exact Fides/Credere and Arbitration returns; parent T20 owns whole-mytheme work. Diabole naming remains authored.

**Return:** Movement → named restored A/C bodies → exact E operation and HISTORY/source → revised Movement at its existing position. No copied full field or new linear sequence.

Under QL's grammar the same puzzle re-derives. The pieces become local covers *of* the topological domain, each known in relation to the quotient and to the covering activity itself — winding and traversal against an infinite capacity for traversal — so the regulating whole is no longer a picture on a box but the covering relation as such, the bounded surface regulated by the infinite plane, `0/1`. The image travels from problem to solution because the essay does not renounce world-pictures; it re-founds them, and [[41-s5-p4-bimba-energy-fields|§5 · #4]] builds the re-founded picture as the Bimba map. There is no picture on the arche-topos's box, because what it holds is the principle of imaging itself; the torus and the atlas are its nearest approximations, and their nearness is derivable rather than asserted.

### T21-M45

[45-s50-p2-antikythera-attunement.md](</Users/admin/Central/Work/O-I/Antykathera-Essay-Work/submission-package/essay/section-rooms/07-instrument-returns/movements/45-s50-p2-antikythera-attunement.md>) · `E3-logos` · A: A15, A16, A17, A35 · C: C20, C48, C51, C62.

> The image gathers two recoveries. The first belongs to the word technē, in the genealogy Heidegger recovered: technē "linked with the word epistēmē," both "names for knowing in the widest sense"; technē belonging "to bringing-forth, to poiēsis"; "it is as revealing, and not as manufacturing, that technē is a bringing-forth" (*QCT*, pp. 12–13). The definition of poiēsis he quotes is Diotima's, from *Symposium* 205b — "every occasion for whatever passes over and goes forward into presencing from that which is not presencing is poiēsis" — and the same banquet gives the essay its symbolon in Aristophanes' speech: emission and return seated at one table from the beginning (the connection is the essay's own, **Argued**). An attunement instrument is technē in this recovered sense — a made thing whose making reveals, coordinating cycles it does not own for an observer it does not replace — which is the mechanism's exact difference from the net of §2 · #3: both are consummate craft; one renders cycles mutually readable for a situated observer, the other renders persons capturable for a convened audience.

**Exact alignment scope:** Retain the late attunement image and independent Freeth/Heidegger claims. Add A15–17/A35 and exact technē/Logos relation; ancient mechanism is not a QL or AI architecture proof.

**Return:** Movement → named restored A/C bodies → exact E operation and HISTORY/source → revised Movement at its existing position. No copied full field or new linear sequence.

## The one complete admitted aphorism

> The approachable limit is the basis of investigation;  
> the absolute limit is the basis of faith;  
> the limit of investigation is the limit of determination;  
> the limit of faith is never to be determined.

E4 **Trust keeps the return route active** and E3 **Fides/Credere — entrusting under non-possession** contribute register3 operations. A03 returns the form to result versus determining condition, A23 to conscious continuation and answerable inquiry, and M04 to its source-distinct genealogy. The full trust/faith/formal-limit transverse path was read; psychic, political, infrastructural and technical offices stay distinct.

Keep all four lines intact. No extra lines or six-slot redistribution. Parent owns future T22/body/consumer work; no manuscript insertion occurred. Both exact routes are recorded as `T21-aphorism-1` and `T21-aphorism-2`, one identity.
